     1→---
     2→name: vercel-react-best-practices
     3→description: React and Next.js performance optimization guidelines from Vercel Engineering. This skill should be used when writing, reviewing, or refactoring React/Next.js code to ensure optimal performance patterns. Triggers on tasks involving React components, Next.js pages, data fetching, bundle optimization, or performance improvements.
     4→license: MIT
     5→metadata:
     6→  author: vercel
     7→  version: "1.0.0"
     8→---
     9→
    10→# Vercel React Best Practices
    11→
    12→Comprehensive performance optimization guide for React and Next.js applications, maintained by Vercel. Contains 64 rules across 8 categories, prioritized by impact to guide automated refactoring and code generation.
    13→
    14→## When to Apply
    15→
    16→Reference these guidelines when:
    17→- Writing new React components or Next.js pages
    18→- Implementing data fetching (client or server-side)
    19→- Reviewing code for performance issues
    20→- Refactoring existing React/Next.js code
    21→- Optimizing bundle size or load times
    22→
    23→## Rule Categories by Priority
    24→
    25→| Priority | Category | Impact | Prefix |
    26→|----------|----------|--------|--------|
    27→| 1 | Eliminating Waterfalls | CRITICAL | `async-` |
    28→| 2 | Bundle Size Optimization | CRITICAL | `bundle-` |
    29→| 3 | Server-Side Performance | HIGH | `server-` |
    30→| 4 | Client-Side Data Fetching | MEDIUM-HIGH | `client-` |
    31→| 5 | Re-render Optimization | MEDIUM | `rerender-` |
    32→| 6 | Rendering Performance | MEDIUM | `rendering-` |
    33→| 7 | JavaScript Performance | LOW-MEDIUM | `js-` |
    34→| 8 | Advanced Patterns | LOW | `advanced-` |
    35→
    36→## Quick Reference
    37→
    38→### 1. Eliminating Waterfalls (CRITICAL)
    39→
    40→- `async-defer-await` - Move await into branches where actually used
    41→- `async-parallel` - Use Promise.all() for independent operations
    42→- `async-dependencies` - Use better-all for partial dependencies
    43→- `async-api-routes` - Start promises early, await late in API routes
    44→- `async-suspense-boundaries` - Use Suspense to stream content
    45→
    46→### 2. Bundle Size Optimization (CRITICAL)
    47→
    48→- `bundle-barrel-imports` - Import directly, avoid barrel files
    49→- `bundle-dynamic-imports` - Use next/dynamic for heavy components
    50→- `bundle-defer-third-party` - Load analytics/logging after hydration
    51→- `bundle-conditional` - Load modules only when feature is activated
    52→- `bundle-preload` - Preload on hover/focus for perceived speed
    53→
    54→### 3. Server-Side Performance (HIGH)
    55→
    56→- `server-auth-actions` - Authenticate server actions like API routes
    57→- `server-cache-react` - Use React.cache() for per-request deduplication
    58→- `server-cache-lru` - Use LRU cache for cross-request caching
    59→- `server-dedup-props` - Avoid duplicate serialization in RSC props
    60→- `server-hoist-static-io` - Hoist static I/O (fonts, logos) to module level
    61→- `server-serialization` - Minimize data passed to client components
    62→- `server-parallel-fetching` - Restructure components to parallelize fetches
    63→- `server-after-nonblocking` - Use after() for non-blocking operations
    64→
    65→### 4. Client-Side Data Fetching (MEDIUM-HIGH)
    66→
    67→- `client-swr-dedup` - Use SWR for automatic request deduplication
    68→- `client-event-listeners` - Deduplicate global event listeners
    69→- `client-passive-event-listeners` - Use passive listeners for scroll
    70→- `client-localstorage-schema` - Version and minimize localStorage data
    71→
    72→### 5. Re-render Optimization (MEDIUM)
    73→
    74→- `rerender-defer-reads` - Don't subscribe to state only used in callbacks
    75→- `rerender-memo` - Extract expensive work into memoized components
    76→- `rerender-memo-with-default-value` - Hoist default non-primitive props
    77→- `rerender-dependencies` - Use primitive dependencies in effects
    78→- `rerender-derived-state` - Subscribe to derived booleans, not raw values
    79→- `rerender-derived-state-no-effect` - Derive state during render, not effects
    80→- `rerender-functional-setstate` - Use functional setState for stable callbacks
    81→- `rerender-lazy-state-init` - Pass function to useState for expensive values
    82→- `rerender-simple-expression-in-memo` - Avoid memo for simple primitives
    83→- `rerender-split-combined-hooks` - Split hooks with independent dependencies
    84→- `rerender-move-effect-to-event` - Put interaction logic in event handlers
    85→- `rerender-transitions` - Use startTransition for non-urgent updates
    86→- `rerender-use-deferred-value` - Defer expensive renders to keep input responsive
    87→- `rerender-use-ref-transient-values` - Use refs for transient frequent values
    88→- `rerender-no-inline-components` - Don't define components inside components
    89→
    90→### 6. Rendering Performance (MEDIUM)
    91→
    92→- `rendering-animate-svg-wrapper` - Animate div wrapper, not SVG element
    93→- `rendering-content-visibility` - Use content-visibility for long lists
    94→- `rendering-hoist-jsx` - Extract static JSX outside components
    95→- `rendering-svg-precision` - Reduce SVG coordinate precision
    96→- `rendering-hydration-no-flicker` - Use inline script for client-only data
    97→- `rendering-hydration-suppress-warning` - Suppress expected mismatches
    98→- `rendering-activity` - Use Activity component for show/hide
    99→- `rendering-conditional-render` - Use ternary, not && for conditionals
   100→- `rendering-usetransition-loading` - Prefer useTransition for loading state
   101→- `rendering-resource-hints` - Use React DOM resource hints for preloading
   102→- `rendering-script-defer-async` - Use defer or async on script tags
   103→
   104→### 7. JavaScript Performance (LOW-MEDIUM)
   105→
   106→- `js-batch-dom-css` - Group CSS changes via classes or cssText
   107→- `js-index-maps` - Build Map for repeated lookups
   108→- `js-cache-property-access` - Cache object properties in loops
   109→- `js-cache-function-results` - Cache function results in module-level Map
   110→- `js-cache-storage` - Cache localStorage/sessionStorage reads
   111→- `js-combine-iterations` - Combine multiple filter/map into one loop
   112→- `js-length-check-first` - Check array length before expensive comparison
   113→- `js-early-exit` - Return early from functions
   114→- `js-hoist-regexp` - Hoist RegExp creation outside loops
   115→- `js-min-max-loop` - Use loop for min/max instead of sort
   116→- `js-set-map-lookups` - Use Set/Map for O(1) lookups
   117→- `js-tosorted-immutable` - Use toSorted() for immutability
   118→- `js-flatmap-filter` - Use flatMap to map and filter in one pass
   119→
   120→### 8. Advanced Patterns (LOW)
   121→
   122→- `advanced-event-handler-refs` - Store event handlers in refs
   123→- `advanced-init-once` - Initialize app once per app load
   124→- `advanced-use-latest` - useLatest for stable callback refs
   125→
   126→## How to Use
   127→
   128→Read individual rule files for detailed explanations and code examples:
   129→
   130→```
   131→rules/async-parallel.md
   132→rules/bundle-barrel-imports.md
   133→```
   134→
   135→Each rule file contains:
   136→- Brief explanation of why it matters
   137→- Incorrect code example with explanation
   138→- Correct code example with explanation
   139→- Additional context and references
   140→
   141→## Full Compiled Document
   142→
   143→For the complete guide with all rules expanded: `AGENTS.md`
   144→

{"additions":5107,"body":"### Stakeholder Overview\n\nAdd a reusable trending-products recommendations carousel to `@customink/pigment-extensions/reco`. Consumers can drop in `<TrendingProductsCarousel categoryId={574} />` with no further configuration — the component fetches its own data. The widget layer (`ProductsCarouselWidget`) is also exported standalone for teams that manage their own data fetching. All components are fully tested and documented in Storybook.\n\n### Affected areas\n\n- 📖 `apps/storybook`\n- 𐂷 npm workspace root, docs, and others\n\n### Risk Estimate\n\n- ✅ Negligible risk!\n\nNew package (`packages/extensions`) — no existing consumers, no shared state changes.\n\n### Changes\n\n#### Top-level components\n\n- **`TrendingProductsCarousel`** — zero-config connected component. Only `categoryId` required; wraps `useTrendingProductsByCategory` + `ProductsCarouselWidget`. Props: `onError`, `renderFavoriteButton`, `renderPriceDetails`, `onCardClicked`, `onWidgetViewed`, `excludeProductIds`, `imageDesignCid`, `slidesConfig`, `enabled`, `children`, `ref`.\n- **`ProductsCarouselWidget`** — generic responsive keen-slider carousel for product cards. Decoupled from data fetching; accepts `products` + `isLoading` directly. Same render props API as `TrendingProductsCarousel` (minus fetch-related props).\n- **`FavoriteButton`** — heart-toggle overlay component for product card images. Controlled — consuming app owns favorites state.\n\n#### Hooks\n\n- **`useTrendingProductsByCategory`** — React Query hook that fetches from the recommendations service by `categoryId`. Supports `limit`, `excludeProductIds`, `imageDesignCid` (logo placement), `baseUrl`, `enabled`.\n- **`useProductCardPriceDetails`** — lazy-fetches pricing quotes from `/products/quotes/new` for a given `styleId` + quantity. Powers `ProductCardPriceWithDetails`.\n\n#### Atoms (`ProductsCarouselWidget/Atoms/`)\n\n- **`Carousel`** — keen-slider wrapper with responsive `perView`/`spacing` config (`slidesConfig`), prev/next arrows, and an `IntersectionObserver`-based `onVisible` callback.\n- **`LoadingSkeleton`** — animated placeholder shown while products are loading. Renders N shimmer card slots matching the `perView` count.\n- **`Popover`** — accessible floating popover (used for pricing details). Positioned relative to trigger; closes on outside click/Escape.\n\n#### Molecules (`ProductsCarouselWidget/Molecules/`)\n\n- **`RecommendationsProductCard`** — product card with image, name, color count, min qty, and price. Accepts `renderFavoriteButton` and `renderPriceDetails` render props for optional overlays.\n- **`ProductCardPriceWithDetails`** — replaces the default price display with a formatted unit price + lazy-loaded \"Pricing Details\" popover (triggered on hover/focus). Uses `useProductCardPriceDetails`.\n- **`ProductCardPriceDetailsContent`** — pure presentational table inside the popover: shows price tiers (qty → unit price) from the quotes API response.\n- **`ProductCardPriceDetailsConnected`** — wires `useProductCardPriceDetails` to `ProductCardPriceDetailsContent`; rendered lazily inside the popover on first open.\n\n#### API layer (`reco/api/`)\n\n- **`mapper.ts`** — maps raw API response to typed `RecommendationProduct[]`; applies `processImageUrl` when `imageDesignCid` is provided.\n- **`imageUtils.ts`** — `processImageUrl` appends logo-placement query params to product image URLs.\n- **`types.ts`** — shared TypeScript types for API response and domain model.\n\n#### Storybook\n\n- 11 stories for `TrendingProductsCarousel`: `Default`, `WithLimit`, `WithYDHImages`, `WithCardClickHandler`, `WithWidgetViewedHandler`, `WithFavoriteButton`, `WithPricingDetails`, `WithViewAllLink`, `WithCustomSlidesConfig`, `WithExcludedProducts`, `WithErrorHandler`, `FetchDisabled`.\n- Stories for `ProductsCarouselWidget`, `FavoriteButton`, all atoms (`Carousel`, `LoadingSkeleton`, `Popover`) and molecules (`RecommendationsProductCard`, `ProductCardPriceWithDetails`, `ProductCardPriceDetailsContent`).\n- All stories use `fetchMock` — no real network calls. Added a `fetchMock` Storybook decorator in `apps/storybook/.storybook/decorator.tsx` that intercepts `window.fetch` by URL substring.\n\n#### Config fixes\n\n- `tsconfig.build.json` — added missing `@customink/design-tokens/semantics/units` path alias that caused CI build failure.\n- `tsconfig.json` — added test file includes and `testing-library__jest-dom` types.\n\n### Project Link\n\nRECO-730 → https://customink.atlassian.net/browse/RECO-730\n\n### Screenshots\n\n\n\n### Testing Instructions\n\n- ✅ ⛮  CI pipelines passing\n- ✅ 📖 Storybook manually verified\n- ✅ 🕵 All AI-generated code +
+/**
+ * Lightweight fetch mock decorator — no MSW required.
+ *
+ * In any story, set `parameters.fetchMock` to a map of URL substring → response body:
+ *
+ * ```ts
+ * parameters: {
+ *   fetchMock: {
+ *     '/products/quotes/new': MOCK_QUOTES_API_RESPONSE,
+ *   },
+ * },
+ * ```
+ *
+ * Requests whose URL contains the key are intercepted and return the mock JSON.
+ * All other requests fall through to the real `window.fetch`.
+ * The original fetch is restored when the story unmounts.
+ */
+const FetchMockWrapper = ({ Story, handlers }: { Story: React.ComponentType; handlers: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
+  // Patch synchronously during render so the mock is in place before child effects fire.
+  // (React runs child useEffects before parent useEffects, so patching in useEffect is too late.)
+  const originalFetchRef = React.useRef(window.fetch);
+  window.fetch = async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
+    const url = input.toString();
+    for (const [pattern, responseBody] of Object.entries(handlers)) {
+      if (url.includes(pattern)) {
+        return new Response(JSON.stringify(responseBody), {
+          status: 200,
+          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+        });
+      }
+    }
+    return originalFetchRef.current(input, init);
+  };
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    return () => {
+      window.fetch = originalFetchRef.current;
+    };
+  }, []);
+
+  return <Story />;
...

   693→+    defaultQuoteQty: p.defaultQuoteQty,
   694→+  };
   695→+}

  1187→+import { useState } from 'react';
  1188→+import type { Meta, StoryObj } from '@storybook/react';
  1189→+import {
  1190→+  TrendingProductsCarousel,
  1191→+  FavoriteButton,

  1196→+import { MOCK_QUOTES_API_RESPONSE } from '@customink/pigment-extensions/reco/mocks/priceDetails';
  1197→+
  1198→+/**
  1199→+ * `TrendingProductsCarousel` is the zero-config entry point for the recommendations carousel.
  1200→+ * It combines `useTrendingProductsByCategory` + `ProductsCarouselWidget` so consumers only
  1201→+ * need to provide `categoryId`.
  1202→+ *
  1203→+ * ```tsx
  1204→+ * <TrendingProductsCarousel categoryId={574} />
  1205→+ * ```
  1206→+ */
  1207→+const meta: Meta<typeof TrendingProductsCarousel> = {
  1208→+  title: 'Extensions/Reco/TrendingProductsCarousel',
  1209→+  component: TrendingProductsCarousel,
  1210→+  parameters: {
  1211→+    layout: 'padded',
  1212→+    // Mock the recommendations API for all stories — no real network calls.
  1213→+    fetchMock: {
  1214→+      'trending-products/by-category': MOCK_API_RESPONSE,
  1215→+    },
  1216→+  },
  1217→+  decorators: [
  1218→+    (Story) => (
  1219→+      <div style={{ marginTop: 20, marginBottom: 20 }}>
  1220→+        <Story />
  1221→+      </div>
  1222→+    ),
  1223→+  ],
  1224→+  args: {
  1225→+    categoryId: 574,
  1226→+    title: 'Trending Products',
  1227→+  },
  1228→+};
  1229→+
  1230→+export default meta;
  1231→+type Story = StoryObj<typeof TrendingProductsCarousel>;
  1232→+
  1233→+/** Minimal usage — only `categoryId` is required. */
  1234→+export const Default: Story = {};
  1235→+
  1236→+/**
  1237→+ * `limit` controls how many products the API returns.
  1238→+ * Useful when the page layout has space for fewer cards.
  1239→+ */
  1240→+export const WithLimit: Story = {
  1241→+  name: 'With Limit',
  1242→+  args: {
  1243→+    limit: 6,
  1244→+    title: 'Top Picks',
  1245→+  },
  1246→+};
  1247→+
  1248→+/**
  1249→+ * `imageDesignCid` applies logo-placement query params to every product image URL.
  1250→+ * Pass `DEFAULT_RECOMMENDATIONS_DESIGN_CID` for the YDH logo, or a real design CID.
  1251→+ */
  1252→+export const WithYDHImages: Story = {
  1253→+  name: 'With YDH Images',
  1254→+  args: {
  1255→+    imageDesignCid: DEFAULT_RECOMMENDATIONS_DESIGN_CID,
  1256→+  },
  1257→+};
  1258→+
  1259→+/**
  1260→+ * `onCardClicked` fires with the product and its 0-based index.
  1261→+ * Use for analytics or navigation. Check the Actions panel.
  1262→+ */
  1263→+export const WithCardClickHandler: Story = {
  1264→+  name: 'With Card Click Handler',
  1265→+  args: {
  1266→+    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  1267→+    onCardClicked: (product, index) => console.log('Card clicked', { index, styleId: product.styleId, name: product.name }),
  1268→+  },
  1269→+};
  1270→+
  1271→+/**
  1272→+ * `onWidgetViewed` fires once when the carousel scrolls into the viewport.
  1273→+ * Use for impression analytics. Check the Actions panel.
  1274→+ */
  1275→+export const WithWidgetViewedHandler: Story = {
  1276→+  name: 'With Widget Viewed Handler',
  1277→+  args: {
  1278→+    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  1279→+    onWidgetViewed: (products) =>
  1280→+      console.log(
  1281→+        'Widget viewed',
  1282→+        products.map((p) => p.styleId),
  1283→+      ),
  1284→+  },
  1285→+};
  1286→+
  1287→+/**
  1288→+ * `renderFavoriteButton` injects a heart toggle overlay on each product image.
  1289→+ * The consuming app owns favorites state.
  1290→+ */
  1291→+export const WithFavoriteButton: Story = {
  1292→+  name: 'With Favorite Button',
  1293→+  render: (args) => {
  1294→+    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/rules-of-hooks
  1295→+    const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  1296→+
  1297→+    return (
  1298→+      <TrendingProductsCarousel
  1299→+        {...args}
  1300→+        // eslint-disable-next-line react/jsx-no-bind
  1301→+        renderFavoriteButton={(product) => (
  1302→+          <FavoriteButton
  1303→+            isFavorited={favorites.has(product.styleId)}
  1304→+            // eslint-disable-next-line react/jsx-no-bind
  1305→+            onClick={(e) => {
  1306→+              e.stopPropagation();
  1307→+              setFavorites((prev) => {
  1308→+                const next = new Set(prev);
  1309→+                if (next.has(product.styleId)) next.delete(product.styleId);
  1310→+                else next.add(product.styleId);
  1311→+                return next;
  1312→+              });
  1313→+            }}
  1314→+          />
  1315→+        )}
  1316→+      />
  1317→+    );
  1318→+  },
  1319→+};
  1320→+
  1321→+/**
  1322→+ * `renderPriceDetails` with `ProductCardPriceWithDetails` — replaces the default price

  1439→+# RECO-730: TrendingProductsWidget Migration Plan
  1440→+
  1441→+**Branch:** `RECO-730-trendng-products-migration`
  1442→+**Package:** `@customink/pigment-extensions/reco`
  1443→+**Source:** `catalog/app/javascript/features/TrendingProducts/TrendingCarousel.jsx`
  1444→+**References:** [ADR-20](../adr/20-Extensions-Package.md) · [RFC-1](../rfc/1-trending-products-widget-migration.md) · PR #1068 · PR #1083
  1445→+
  1446→+---
  1447→+
  1448→+## Scope Summary
  1449→+
  1450→+Migrate `TrendingCarousel.jsx` from the catalog repo into `packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsWidget/` using the ADR-20 extensions structure. The widget is broken into internal atoms/molecules (not publicly exported) and one assembled widget component + one dumb hook (publicly exported).
  1451→+
  1452→+---
  1453→+
  1454→+## What Does NOT Move
  1455→+
  1456→+| Catalog piece | Why | How replaced |
  1457→+|---|---|---|
  1458→+| `FavoritesProvider` / `useFavorites` | Auth-aware, catalog-specific context | Catalog wraps widget with `FavoritesProvider`; injects button via `renderFavoriteButton` render prop |
  1459→+| `ProductCardPriceDetails` | Deeply catalog-specific: Rollbar, AB tests, `useProductCardPriceDetails` data fetching | `RecommendationsProductCard` exposes `renderPriceDetails` render prop |
  1460→+| `useTrendingProductsWrapper` / `usePromotionalProducts` | React Query, catalog API config | Replaced by dumb `useTrendingProducts` hook that accepts a `fetcher` function |
  1461→+| `firePromoTrendingRecommendationClickEvent` / `ViewEvent` | Catalog analytics utils | Replaced by `onWidgetViewed` / `onCardViewed` / `onCardClicked` callback props on the widget |
  1462→+| `useBreakpoint` (catalog hook) | Catalog-specific | Re-implement as local `useMediaQuery`-based hook inside widget |
  1463→+| MUI `styled()` | v0 pattern, no MUI in new code | CSS Modules |
  1464→+
  1465→+---
  1466→+
  1467→+## File Structure
  1468→+
  1469→+```
  1470→+packages/extensions/reco/
  1471→+  TrendingProductsWidget/
  1472→+    TrendingProductsWidget.tsx          # assembled widget, public
  1473→+    TrendingProductsWidget.module.css
  1474→+    TrendingProductsWidget.test.tsx
  1475→+    types.ts                            # all TypeScript contracts
  1476→+    index.ts                            # public exports only
  1477→+    Atoms/                              # internal, NOT exported from index.ts
  1478→+      Carousel/
  1479→+        Carousel.tsx
  1480→+        Carousel.module.css
  1481→+        index.ts
  1482→+      FavoriteButton/
  1483→+        FavoriteButton.tsx
  1484→+        FavoriteButton.module.css
  1485→+        index.ts

  1488→+        index.ts

  1491→+        LoadingSkeleton.module.css
  1492→+        index.ts

  1585→+- `useResizeObserver` (`@react-hook/resize-observer`) — check if already in extensions deps; if not, inline a `ResizeObserver` effect

  1675→+
  1676→+## useTrendingProducts Hook (public)
  1677→+
  1678→+```ts
  1679→+type TrendingProductsFetcher = () => Promise<TrendingProduct[]>;
  1680→+
  1681→+type UseTrendingProductsOptions = {
  1682→+  fetcher: TrendingProductsFetcher;
  1683→+  enabled?: boolean;
  1684→+};
  1685→+
  1686→+type UseTrendingProductsResult = {
  1687→+  products: TrendingProduct[];
  1688→+  isLoading: boolean;
  1689→+  isError: boolean;
  1690→+};
  1691→+
  1692→+function useTrendingProducts(options: UseTrendingProductsOptions): UseTrendingProductsResult;
  1693→+```
  1694→+
  1695→+**Implementation notes:**
  1696→+- Dumb hook: calls `fetcher()`, manages `loading`/`error`/`data` state with `useEffect` + `useState`
  1697→+- No React Query, no catalog API wiring — those stay in catalog
  1698→+- No in-memory cache for phase 1 (simple `useEffect`, re-fetches on mount)
  1699→+- `enabled` flag: skip fetch when `false` (matches catalog `useTrendingProducts` pattern)
  1700→+- Returns empty `products: []` on error (same behavior as current catalog hook)
  1701→+
  1702→+**Catalog usage:**
  1703→+```ts
  1704→+// catalog: useTrendingProductsWrapper.js wraps this
  1705→+const { products, isLoading } = useTrendingProducts({
  1706→+  fetcher: () => fetchPromotionalProducts({ categoryId, limit: 20 }),
  1707→+  enabled: true,
  1708→+});
  1709→+```
  1710→+
  1711→+---
  1712→+
  1713→+## TrendingProductsWidget Assembly
  1714→+
  1715→+**Responsive slidesPerView logic** (preserve from `TrendingCarousel.jsx`):
  1716→+
  1717→+| Breakpoint | Products count | slidesPerView | spacing |
  1718→+|---|---|---|---|
  1719→+| Mobile (`≤ 768px`) | ≥ 3 | 1.6 | 8px |
  1720→+| Mobile | < 3 | `'auto'` | 8px |
  1721→+| Desktop | ≥ 5 | 4 | 16px |
  1722→+| Desktop | < 5 | `'auto'` | 16px |
  1723→+
  1724→+**Arrow visibility:** `showArrows = slidesPerView !== 'auto' && !isMobile`
  1725→+
  1726→+**Widget behavior:**
  1727→+- `isLoading` → render `LoadingSkeleton`
  1728→+- `products.length === 0` → return `null`
  1729→+- Viewport visibility: `IntersectionObserver` on wrapper → `onWidgetViewed(products)` fires once
  1730→+- Card click → `onCardClicked(product, index)`
  1731→+- Card in viewport → `onCardViewed(product, index)` (per-card IntersectionObserver, optional phase 2)
  1732→+
  1733→+**`FavoritesProvider` is NOT wrapped here** — catalog is responsible for wrapping:
  1734→+```tsx
  1735→+// catalog (after migration)
  1736→+<FavoritesProvider pageType={PageType.SUBCATEGORY}>
  1737→+  <TrendingProductsWidget
  1738→+    products={products}
  1739→+    isLoading={isLoading}
  1740→+    renderFavoriteButton={(product) => (
  1741→+      <FavoriteButton
  1742→+        styleId={product.styleId}
  1743→+        isFavorited={isFavorited(product.styleId)}
  1744→+        onToggle={...}
  1745→+      />
  1746→+    )}
  1747→+    renderPriceDetails={(product) => (
  1748→+      <ProductCardPriceDetails
  1749→+        defaultUnitPrices={product.defaultUnitPrices}
  1750→+        productId={product.styleId}
  1751→+        quantity={product.defaultQuoteQty}
  1752→+        {...catalogSpecificProps}
  1753→+      />
  1754→+    )}
  1755→+  />
  1756→+</FavoritesProvider>
  1757→+```
  1758→+
  1759→+---
  1760→+
  1761→+## Public Exports (`reco/index.ts`)
  1762→+
  1763→+```ts
  1764→+export { TrendingProductsWidget } from './TrendingProductsWidget';
  1765→+export { useTrendingProducts } from './useTrendingProducts';
  1766→+export type { TrendingProduct, TrendingProductsWidgetProps } from './TrendingProductsWidget/types';
  1767→+```
  1768→+
  1769→+Atoms and Molecules are **NOT exported**. No deep imports from catalog.

  1788→+## Implementation Phases
  1789→+
  1790→+### Phase 0 — Dependency audit (before coding)
  1791→+- [ ] Open DS review request for `keen-slider` in `packages/extensions/package.json`

  1919→+describe('FavoriteButton', () => {

  1922→+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add to favorites' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  1923→+  });
  1924→+
  1925→+  it('renders "Remove from favorites" label when favorited', () => {
  1926→+    render(<FavoriteButton isFavorited={true} onClick={() => {}} />);
  1927→+    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove from favorites' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  1928→+  });
  1929→+
  1930→+  it('sets aria-pressed to false when not favorited', () => {
  1931→+    render(<FavoriteButton isFavorited={false} onClick={() => {}} />);
  1932→+    expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  1933→+  });
  1934→+
  1935→+  it('sets aria-pressed to true when favorited', () => {
  1936→+    render(<FavoriteButton isFavorited={true} onClick={() => {}} />);
  1937→+    expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
  1938→+  });
  1939→+
  1940→+  it('calls onClick when clicked', () => {
  1941→+    const onClick = jest.fn();
  1942→+    render(<FavoriteButton isFavorited={false} onClick={onClick} />);
  1943→+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button'));
  1944→+    expect(onClick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  1945→+  });
  1946→+
  1947→+  it('forwards ref to the button element', () => {
  1948→+    const ref = createRef<HTMLButtonElement>();
  1949→+    render(<FavoriteButton isFavorited={false} onClick={() => {}} ref={ref} />);
  1950→+    expect(ref.current).toBeInstanceOf(HTMLButtonElement);
  1951→+  });
  1952→+
  1953→+  it('applies custom className', () => {
  1954→+    const ref = createRef<HTMLButtonElement>();
  1955→+    render(<FavoriteButton isFavorited={false} onClick={() => {}} className="custom" ref={ref} />);
  1956→+    expect(ref.current?.className).toContain('custom');
  1957→+  });
  1958→+});

  2262→+import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
  2263→+import { createRef } from 'react';
  2264→+import { LoadingSkeleton } from './LoadingSkeleton';
  2265→+
  2266→+describe('LoadingSkeleton', () => {
  2267→+  it('renders the skeleton container', () => {
  2268→+    render(<LoadingSkeleton />);
  2269→+    expect(screen.getByTestId('carousel-skeleton')).toBeInTheDocument();
  2270→+  });
  2271→+
  2272→+  it('renders 4 card placeholders by default', () => {
  2273→+    const { container } = render(<LoadingSkeleton />);
  2274→+    const cardsGrid = container.querySelector('[data-testid="carousel-skeleton"] > div:last-child');
  2275→+    expect(cardsGrid?.children).toHaveLength(4);
  2276→+  });
  2277→+
  2278→+  it('renders the specified number of card placeholders', () => {
  2279→+    const { container } = render(<LoadingSkeleton count={6} />);
  2280→+    const cardsGrid = container.querySelector('[data-testid="carousel-skeleton"] > div:last-child');
  2281→+    expect(cardsGrid?.children).toHaveLength(6);
  2282→+  });
  2283→+
  2284→+  it('forwards ref to the root element', () => {
  2285→+    const ref = createRef<HTMLDivElement>();
  2286→+    render(<LoadingSkeleton ref={ref} />);
  2287→+    expect(ref.current).toBe(screen.getByTestId('carousel-skeleton'));
  2288→+  });
  2289→+
  2290→+  it('applies custom className', () => {
  2291→+    render(<LoadingSkeleton className="custom" />);
  2292→+    expect(screen.getByTestId('carousel-skeleton').className).toContain('custom');
  2293→+  });
  2294→+});

  2301→+import React from 'react';
  2302→+import styles from './LoadingSkeleton.module.css';
  2303→+
  2304→+export type LoadingSkeletonProps = {
  2305→+  /** Number of card placeholders to render. Should match the current breakpoint's perView. */
  2306→+  count?: number;
  2307→+  /** Gap between cards in pixels. Should match the current breakpoint's spacing. */
  2308→+  gap?: number;
  2309→+  className?: string;
  2310→+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
  2311→+};
  2312→+
  2313→+/** Shimmer placeholder for the carousel. Ported from CarouselSkeleton in catalog's TrendingProducts feature. */
  2314→+export const LoadingSkeleton = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, LoadingSkeletonProps>(function LoadingSkeleton(
  2315→+  { count = 4, gap = 8, className, style },
  2316→+  ref,
  2317→+) {
  2318→+  const rootClass = [styles.root, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  2319→+
  2320→+  return (
  2321→+    <div ref={ref} className={rootClass} style={style} data-testid="carousel-skeleton">
  2322→+      <div className={`${styles.shimmer} ${styles.header}`} />
  2323→+      <div className={styles.cards} style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${count}, 1fr)`, gap: `${gap}px` }}>
  2324→+        {Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => (
  2325→+          <div key={i} className={`${styles.shimmer} ${styles.card}`}>
  2326→+            <div className={styles.cardContent}>
  2327→+              <div className={`${styles.shimmer} ${styles.cardTitle}`} />
  2328→+              <div className={`${styles.shimmer} ${styles.cardPrice}`} />
  2329→+            </div>
  2330→+          </div>
  2331→+        ))}
  2332→+      </div>
  2333→+    </div>
  2334→+  );
  2335→+});
  2336→+
  2337→+LoadingSkeleton.displayName = 'LoadingSkeleton';

  2406→diff --git a/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/Atoms/Popover/Popover.tsx b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/Atoms/Popover/Popover.tsx
  2407→new file mode 100644
  2408→index 000000000..f9489c138
  2409→--- /dev/null
  2410→+++ b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/Atoms/Popover/Popover.tsx
  2411→@@ -0,0 +1,142 @@
  2412→+import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
  2413→+import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
  2414→+import styles from './Popover.module.css';
  2415→+
  2416→+type Side = 'top' | 'bottom' | 'left' | 'right';
  2417→+
  2418→+const GAP_PX = 8;
  2419→+const VP_PAD = 8; // minimum gap between panel edge and viewport edge
  2420→+
  2421→+// Off-screen sentinel: keeps the panel in the DOM for fade-out but outside the viewport.
  2422→+const OFFSCREEN: React.CSSProperties = { position: 'fixed', top: -9999, left: -9999 };
  2423→+
  2424→+function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  2425→+  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
  2426→+}
  2427→+
  2428→+/** Returns a viewport-clamped `position:fixed` style. Reads offsetWidth/offsetHeight while the panel is off-screen for single-pass positioning. */
  2429→+function computeClampedStyle(triggerRect: DOMRect, side: Side, panelW: number, panelH: number): React.CSSProperties {
  2430→+  const vw = window.innerWidth;
  2431→+  const vh = window.innerHeight;
  2432→+
  2433→+  switch (side) {
  2434→+    case 'bottom': {
  2435→+      const left = clamp(triggerRect.left + triggerRect.width / 2, VP_PAD + panelW / 2, vw - VP_PAD - panelW / 2);
  2436→+      const top = clamp(triggerRect.bottom + GAP_PX, VP_PAD, vh - VP_PAD - panelH);
  2437→+      return { position: 'fixed', top, left };
  2438→+    }
  2439→+    case 'top': {
  2440→+      const left = clamp(triggerRect.left + triggerRect.width / 2, VP_PAD + panelW / 2, vw - VP_PAD - panelW / 2);
  2441→+      const top = clamp(triggerRect.top - GAP_PX - panelH, VP_PAD, vh - VP_PAD - panelH);
  2442→+      return { position: 'fixed', top, left };
  2443→+    }
  2444→+    case 'right': {
  2445→+      const left = clamp(triggerRect.right + GAP_PX, VP_PAD, vw - VP_PAD - panelW);
  2446→+      const top = clamp(triggerRect.top + triggerRect.height / 2, VP_PAD + panelH / 2, vh - VP_PAD - panelH / 2);
  2447→+      return { position: 'fixed', left, top };
  2448→+    }
  2449→+    case 'left': {
  2450→+      const left = clamp(triggerRect.left - GAP_PX - panelW, VP_PAD, vw - VP_PAD - panelW);
  2451→+      const top = clamp(triggerRect.top + triggerRect.height / 2, VP_PAD + panelH / 2, vh - VP_PAD - panelH / 2);
  2452→+      return { position: 'fixed', left, top };
  2453→+    }
  2454→+  }
  2455→+}
  2456→+
  2457→+export interface PopoverProps {
  2458→+  /** Rich content rendered inside the popover panel */
  2459→+  content: React.ReactNode;
  2460→+  /** The element that triggers the popover on hover/focus */
  2461→+  children: React.ReactNode;
  2462→+  /**
  2463→+   * Placement relative to the trigger.
  2464→+   * `'auto'` picks the side with the most available viewport space when the popover opens.
  2465→+   * @default 'auto'
  2466→+   */
  2467→+  placement?: Side | 'auto';
  2468→+}
  2469→+
  2470→+/**
  2471→+ * Hover/focus popover. Portals into document.body to avoid overflow clipping.
  2472→+ * Events are on the wrapper span so any child works as a trigger.
  2473→+ */
  2474→+export const Popover = React.forwardRef<HTMLSpanElement, PopoverProps>(function Popover(
  2475→+  { content, children, placement = 'auto' },
  2476→+  ref,
  2477→+) {
  2478→+  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);
  2479→+  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  2480→+
  2481→+  useEffect(() => {
  2482→+    setMounted(true);
  2483→+  }, []);
  2484→+  const [resolvedPlacement, setResolvedPlacement] = useState<Side>('bottom');
  2485→+  const [panelStyle, setPanelStyle] = useState<React.CSSProperties>(OFFSCREEN);
  2486→+
  2487→+  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
  2488→+  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
  2489→+
  2490→+  // Merge forwarded ref with internal ref needed for auto-placement measurement
  2491→+  const setWrapperRef = useCallback(
  2492→+    (node: HTMLSpanElement | null) => {
  2493→+      (wrapperRef as React.MutableRefObject<HTMLSpanElement | null>).current = node;
  2494→+      if (typeof ref === 'function') ref(node);
  2495→+      else if (ref) (ref as React.MutableRefObject<HTMLSpanElement | null>).current = node;
  2496→+    },
  2497→+    // ref identity is stable for the lifetime of the component
  2498→+    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  2499→+    [],
  2500→+  );
  2501→+
  2502→+  const show = useCallback(() => {
  2503→+    if (!wrapperRef.current || !panelRef.current) return;
  2504→+    const triggerRect = wrapperRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  2505→+
  2506→+    let side: Side;
  2507→+    if (placement === 'auto') {
  2508→+      const spaces: Record<Side, number> = {
  2509→+        bottom: window.innerHeight - triggerRect.bottom,
  2510→+        top: triggerRect.top,
  2511→+        right: window.innerWidth - triggerRect.right,
  2512→+        left: triggerRect.left,
  2513→+      };
  2514→+      side = (Object.entries(spaces) as [Side, number][]).reduce((a, b) => (b[1] > a[1] ? b : a))[0];
  2515→+    } else {
  2516→+      side = placement;
  2517→+    }
  2518→+    setResolvedPlacement(side);
  2519→+
  2520→+    const panelW = panelRef.current.offsetWidth;
  2521→+    const panelH = panelRef.current.offsetHeight;
  2522→+    setPanelStyle(computeClampedStyle(triggerRect, side, panelW, panelH));
  2523→+    setVisible(true);
  2524→+  }, [placement]);
  2525→+
  2526→+  const hide = useCallback(() => setVisible(false), []);
  2527→+
  2528→+  const effective = placement === 'auto' ? resolvedPlacement : placement;
  2529→+  const panelClass = [styles.panel, styles[effective], visible ? styles.visible : undefined].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  2530→+
  2531→+  return (
  2532→+    <span
  2533→+      ref={setWrapperRef}
  2534→+      className={styles.wrapper}
  2535→+      onMouseEnter={show}
  2536→+      onMouseLeave={hide}
  2537→+      onFocus={show}
  2538→+      onBlur={hide}
  2539→+    >
  2540→+      {children}
  2541→+      {mounted
  2542→+        ? createPortal(
  2543→+            <span ref={panelRef} role="dialog" aria-hidden={!visible} className={panelClass} style={panelStyle}>
  2544→+              {content}
  2545→+            </span>,
  2546→+            document.body,
  2547→+          )
  2548→+        : null}
  2549→+    </span>
  2550→+  );
  2551→+});
  2552→+
  2553→+Popover.displayName = 'Popover';

  2617→@@ -0,0 +1,61 @@
  2618→+import React, { useCallback } from 'react';
  2619→+import { Typography } from '@customink/pigment-react';
  2620→+import type { UseProductCardPriceDetailsOptions } from '../../../useProductCardPriceDetails';
  2621→+import type { UnitPrice } from '../../types';
  2622→+import { Popover } from '../../Atoms/Popover';
  2623→+import { formatPrice } from '../../utils';
  2624→+import { ProductCardPriceDetailsConnected } from '../ProductCardPriceDetails/ProductCardPriceDetailsConnected';
  2625→+import styles from './ProductCardPriceWithDetails.module.css';
  2626→+
  2627→+export type ProductCardPriceWithDetailsProps = UseProductCardPriceDetailsOptions & {
  2628→+  /** Unit prices used to derive the formatted default price shown alongside the "Pricing Details" button. */
  2629→+  defaultUnitPrices: UnitPrice[];
  2630→+};
  2631→+
  2632→+/**
  2633→+ * Formatted default price + lazy "Pricing Details" popover. Use via `renderPriceDetails` on `ProductsCarouselWidget`:
  2634→+ *
  2635→+ * ```tsx
  2636→+ * <ProductsCarouselWidget
  2637→+ *   renderPriceDetails={(product) => (
  2638→+ *     <ProductCardPriceWithDetails
  2639→+ *       styleId={product.styleId}
  2640→+ *       minQty={product.minQty}
  2641→+ *       defaultUnitPrices={product.defaultUnitPrices}
  2642→+ *       defaultQuoteQty={product.defaultQuoteQty}
  2643→+ *       quotesPath="/products/quotes/new"
  2644→+ *     />
  2645→+ *   )}
  2646→+ * />
  2647→+ * ```
  2648→+ */
  2649→+export function ProductCardPriceWithDetails({ defaultUnitPrices, ...connectedProps }: ProductCardPriceWithDetailsProps) {
  2650→+  const formattedPrice = formatPrice(defaultUnitPrices, connectedProps.defaultQuoteQty);
  2651→+
  2652→+  const stopPropagation = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => e.stopPropagation(), []);
  2653→+
  2654→+  return (
  2655→+    <div className={styles.root}>
  2656→+      {formattedPrice !== null ? (
  2657→+        <Typography variant="uiTextTiny" className={styles.price}>
  2658→+          {formattedPrice.originalAmount != null ? (
  2659→+            <>
  2660→+              <span className={styles.originalPrice}>{formattedPrice.originalAmount}</span>
  2661→+              <span className={styles.discountPrice}>{formattedPrice.amount}</span>
  2662→+            </>
  2663→+          ) : (
  2664→+            <span className={styles.priceAmount}>{formattedPrice.amount}</span>
  2665→+          )}
  2666→+          {formattedPrice.suffix}
  2667→+        </Typography>
  2668→+      ) : null}
  2669→+      <Popover placement="auto" content={<ProductCardPriceDetailsConnected {...connectedProps} />}>
  2670→+        <button type="button" className={styles.pricingDetailsLink} onClick={stopPropagation}>
  2671→+          Pricing Details
  2672→+        </button>
  2673→+      </Popover>
  2674→+    </div>
  2675→+  );
  2676→+}
  2677→+
  2678→+ProductCardPriceWithDetails.displayName = 'ProductCardPriceWithDetails';

  2693→+import React from 'react';
  2694→+import { useProductCardPriceDetails } from '../../../useProductCardPriceDetails';
  2695→+import type { UseProductCardPriceDetailsOptions } from '../../../useProductCardPriceDetails';
  2696→+import { ProductCardPriceDetailsContent } from './ProductCardPriceDetailsContent';
  2697→+
  2698→+/**
  2699→+ * Data-fetching wrapper around ProductCardPriceDetailsContent.
  2700→+ * Fetch is lazy — triggered only when this component mounts (i.e. when the Popover opens).
  2701→+ * Exported from the reco public API for use via renderPriceDetails.
  2702→+ */
  2703→+export function ProductCardPriceDetailsConnected(props: UseProductCardPriceDetailsOptions) {
  2704→+  const { frontColor = 1, backColor = 0 } = props;
  2705→+  const result = useProductCardPriceDetails(props);
  2706→+  return <ProductCardPriceDetailsContent {...result} frontColor={frontColor} backColor={backColor} />;
  2707→+}

  2829→+
  2830→+export type ProductCardPriceDetailsContentProps = {
  2831→+  isLoading: boolean;
  2832→+  isError: boolean;
  2833→+  /** Map of quantity → formatted price string (e.g. "27.44"), null when price unavailable. */
  2834→+  priceDetailsData: Record<number, string | null> | null;
  2835→+  /** Sorted list of quantities shown in the table. */

  2839→+  /** Number of back print colors — used for the design description title. @default 0 */

  2841→+  /** Show the "Save" column with percentage savings relative to the first tier. @default false */

  2843→+};
  2844→+
  2845→+/** Pure presentational price-details panel. Use `ProductCardPriceDetailsConnected` for the data-fetching version. */
  2846→+export function ProductCardPriceDetailsContent({
  2847→+  isLoading,
  2848→+  isError,
  2849→+  priceDetailsData,
  2850→+  quotableQuantities,
  2851→+  frontColor = 1,

  2853→+  showSavings = false,
  2854→+}: ProductCardPriceDetailsContentProps) {
  2855→+  const colorCount = Math.max(frontColor, backColor);
  2856→+  const sideCount = backColor > 0 ? 2 : 1;
  2857→+
  2858→+  const basePrice = priceDetailsData ? Number(priceDetailsData[quotableQuantities[0]]) : null;
  2859→+
  2860→+  if (isError) {
  2861→+    return (
  2862→+      <div className={styles.root}>
  2863→+        <Typography variant="uiTextSmall">Unable to load pricing. Please try again.</Typography>
  2864→+      </div>
  2865→+    );
  2866→+  }
  2867→+
  2868→+  return (
  2869→+    <div className={styles.root}>
  2870→+      <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" className={styles.title}>
  2871→+        For a {colorCount}-color, {sideCount}-sided design:
  2872→+      </Typography>
  2873→+
  2874→+      <table className={styles.table}>
  2875→+        <thead>
  2876→+          <tr className={styles.headerRow}>
  2877→+            <th className={styles.colQty}>
  2878→+              <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" fontWeight="bold" component="span">
  2879→+                Quantity
  2880→+              </Typography>
  2881→+            </th>
  2882→+            {showSavings ? (
  2883→+              <th className={styles.colSave}>
  2884→+                <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" fontWeight="bold" component="span">
  2885→+                  Save
  2886→+                </Typography>
  2887→+              </th>
  2888→+            ) : null}
  2889→+            <th>
  2890→+              <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" fontWeight="bold" component="span">
  2891→+                Price/Item
  2892→+              </Typography>
  2893→+            </th>
  2894→+          </tr>
  2895→+        </thead>
  2896→+        <tbody>
  2897→+          {quotableQuantities.map((qty, index) => {
  2898→+            const priceStr = priceDetailsData?.[qty];
  2899→+            const price = priceStr != null ? Number(priceStr) : null;
  2900→+            const savePercent =
  2901→+              index > 0 && basePrice != null && price != null && basePrice > 0
  2902→+                ? Math.round((1 - price / basePrice) * 100)
  2903→+                : null;
  2904→+
  2905→+            return (
  2906→+              <tr key={qty} className={styles.row}>
  2907→+                <td>
  2908→+                  <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" component="span">
  2909→+                    {qty}
  2910→+                  </Typography>
  2911→+                  {index === 0 ? (
  2912→+                    <Typography variant="uiTextTiny" component="span" className={styles.minLabel}>
  2913→+                      (minimum)
  2914→+                    </Typography>
  2915→+                  ) : null}
  2916→+                </td>
  2917→+                {showSavings ? (
  2918→+                  <td>
  2919→+                    {savePercent != null && savePercent > 0 ? (
  2920→+                      <Typography variant="uiTextTiny" fontWeight="bold" component="span" className={styles.saveBadge}>
  2921→+                        {savePercent}%
  2922→+                      </Typography>
  2923→+                    ) : null}
  2924→+                  </td>
  2925→+                ) : null}
  2926→+                <td>
  2927→+                  {isLoading ? (
  2928→+                    <span className={styles.priceShimmer} aria-hidden="true" />
  2929→+                  ) : priceStr != null ? (
  2930→+                    <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" component="span">
  2931→+                      ${Number(priceStr).toFixed(2)}

  2933→+                  ) : (
  2934→+                    <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" component="span">
  2935→+                      —
  2936→+                    </Typography>
  2937→+                  )}
  2938→+                </td>
  2939→+              </tr>
  2940→+            );
  2941→+          })}
  2942→+        </tbody>
  2943→+      </table>
  2944→+
  2945→+      <div className={styles.footer}>
  2946→+        <Typography variant="uiTextSmall">All-inclusive pricing includes</Typography>

  3311→+/** Internal product card molecule. Not on the public API. */
  3312→+export const RecommendationsProductCard = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, RecommendationsProductCardProps>(
  3313→+  function RecommendationsProductCard(
  3314→+    {
  3315→+      styleId,
  3316→+      name,
  3317→+      linkHref,
  3318→+      imageSrc,
  3319→+      imageSrcSet,

  3322→+      ratingScore,
  3323→+      ratingCount,
  3324→+      defaultUnitPrices,

  3326→+      onClick,
  3327→+      imageOverlay,
  3328→+      renderPriceDetails,
  3329→+      children,
  3330→+      className,
  3331→+      style,

  3333→+    ref,

  3335→+    const hasRating = ratingScore != null && ratingScore > 0 && ratingCount != null && ratingCount > 0;
  3336→+    const formattedPrice = formatPrice(defaultUnitPrices, defaultQuoteQty);
  3337→+    const cardClass = [styles.card, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  3338→+
  3339→+    const handleClick = useCallback(

  3351→+      },
  3352→+      [onClick, styleId],
  3353→+    );

  3356→+      <div
  3357→+        ref={ref}
  3358→+        className={cardClass}
  3359→+        style={style}
  3360→+        role="button"
  3361→+        tabIndex={0}
  3362→+        onClick={handleClick}
  3363→+        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
  3364→+      >
  3365→+        {/* overlay is a sibling to <a> — avoids button-inside-anchor */}
  3366→+        <div className={styles.imageContainer}>

  3374→+        </div>
  3375→+
  3376→+        <div className={styles.content}>

  3379→+              {name}
  3380→+            </Typography>
  3381→+          </a>
  3382→+
  3383→+          {hasRating ? (
  3384→+            <div className={styles.ratingRow}>
  3385→+              <svg
  3386→+                width={20}
  3387→+                height={20}
  3388→+                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
  3389→+                fill="none"
  3390→+                xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
  3391→+                aria-hidden="true"

  3395→+              </svg>
  3396→+              <Typography variant="uiTextSmall" className={styles.ratingScore}>
  3397→+                {formatRatingScore(ratingScore)}
  3398→+              </Typography>
  3399→+              <span className={styles.ratingCount}>{formatRatingCount(ratingCount)}</span>
  3400→+            </div>

  3402→+
  3403→+          <div className={styles.priceArea}>

  3581→+import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
  3582→+import { Typography } from '@customink/pigment-react';
  3583→+import type { ProductsCarouselWidgetProps } from './types';
  3584→+import { Carousel } from './Atoms/Carousel';
  3585→+import { LoadingSkeleton } from './Atoms/LoadingSkeleton';
  3586→+import { RecommendationsProductCard } from './Molecules/RecommendationsProductCard';
  3587→+import { useBreakpoint, getSlidesConfig } from './utils';
  3588→+import styles from './ProductsCarouselWidget.module.css';
  3589→+
  3590→+/**
  3591→+ * Data fetching stays in the consuming app. Inject FavoriteButton via `renderFavoriteButton`
  3592→+ * and wrap with FavoritesProvider in the consuming app.
  3593→+ */
  3594→+export const ProductsCarouselWidget = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, ProductsCarouselWidgetProps>(
  3595→+  function ProductsCarouselWidget(
  3596→+    {
  3597→+      products,
  3598→+      isLoading = false,
  3599→+      title = 'Trending Products',
  3600→+      onWidgetViewed,
  3601→+      onCardClicked,
  3602→+      renderFavoriteButton,
  3603→+      renderPriceDetails,
  3604→+      slidesConfig,
  3605→+      children,
  3606→+      className,
  3607→+      style,
  3608→+    },
  3609→+    ref,
  3610→+  ) {
  3611→+    const breakpoint = useBreakpoint();
  3612→+    const { perView, spacing } = getSlidesConfig(breakpoint, slidesConfig);
  3613→+    const loop = products.length > perView;
  3614→+    const showArrows = loop && breakpoint !== 'mobile';
  3615→+
  3616→+    const widgetRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  3617→+    const hasFiredViewEvent = useRef(false);
  3618→+
  3619→+    useEffect(() => {
  3620→+      const el = widgetRef.current;
  3621→+      if (!el || products.length === 0 || hasFiredViewEvent.current) return;
  3622→+
  3623→+      const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
  3624→+        (entries) => {
  3625→+          entries.forEach((entry) => {
  3626→+            if (entry.isIntersecting && !hasFiredViewEvent.current) {
  3627→+              onWidgetViewed?.(products);
  3628→+              hasFiredViewEvent.current = true;
  3629→+            }
  3630→+          });
  3631→+        },
  3632→+        { threshold: 0.1 },
  3633→+      );
  3634→+
  3635→+      observer.observe(el);
  3636→+      return () => observer.disconnect();
  3637→+    }, [products, onWidgetViewed]);
  3638→+
  3639→+    if (isLoading) return <LoadingSkeleton count={Math.floor(perView)} gap={spacing} />;
  3640→+    if (products.length === 0) return null;
  3641→+
  3642→+    const rootClass = [styles.root, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  3643→+
  3644→+    return (
  3645→+      <div ref={ref} className={rootClass} style={style}>
  3646→+        <div ref={widgetRef}>
  3647→+          <Typography variant="heading3" className={styles.title}>
  3648→+            {title}
  3649→+          </Typography>
  3650→+
  3651→+          <Carousel slidesPerView={perView} spacing={spacing} loop={loop} arrows={showArrows}>
  3652→+            {products.map((product, index) => (
  3653→+              <RecommendationsProductCard
  3654→+                key={product.styleId}
  3655→+                styleId={product.styleId}
  3656→+                name={product.name}
  3657→+                linkHref={product.link}
  3658→+                imageSrc={product.imageSrc}
  3659→+                imageSrcSet={product.imageSrcSet}
  3660→+                imageAlt={product.imageAlt}
  3661→+                minQty={product.minQty}
  3662→+                ratingScore={product.ratingScore}
  3663→+                ratingCount={product.ratingCount}
  3664→+                defaultUnitPrices={product.defaultUnitPrices}
  3665→+                defaultQuoteQty={product.defaultQuoteQty}
  3666→+                // eslint-disable-next-line react/jsx-no-bind
  3667→+                onClick={() => onCardClicked?.(product, index)}
  3668→+                imageOverlay={renderFavoriteButton?.(product)}
  3669→+                // eslint-disable-next-line react/jsx-no-bind
  3670→+                renderPriceDetails={renderPriceDetails ? () => renderPriceDetails(product) : undefined}
  3671→+              />
  3672→+            ))}
  3673→+          </Carousel>
  3674→+
  3675→+          {children}
  3676→+        </div>
  3677→+      </div>
  3678→+    );
  3679→+  },
  3680→+);
  3681→+
  3682→+ProductsCarouselWidget.displayName = 'ProductsCarouselWidget';

  3689→+export { ProductsCarouselWidget } from './ProductsCarouselWidget';
  3690→+export type { ProductsCarouselWidgetProps, TrendingProduct, UnitPrice } from './types';
  3691→diff --git a/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/types.ts b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/types.ts
  3692→new file mode 100644
  3693→index 000000000..20d758ec4
  3694→--- /dev/null
  3695→+++ b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/types.ts
  3696→@@ -0,0 +1,66 @@
  3697→+import type React from 'react';
  3698→+export type Breakpoint = 'mobile' | 'tablet' | 'desktop';
  3699→+
  3700→+export type UnitPrice = {
  3701→+  quantity: number;
  3702→+  price: number;
  3703→+  /** Original price before discount. When present and greater than `price`, a strikethrough discount display is shown. */
  3704→+  regularPrice?: number;
  3705→+};
  3706→+
  3707→+export type TrendingProduct = {
  3708→+  styleId: number;
  3709→+  name: string;
  3710→+  link: string;
  3711→+  imageSrc: string;
  3712→+  imageSrcSet?: string;
  3713→+  imageAlt?: string;
  3714→+  minQty: number;
  3715→+  ratingScore?: number;
  3716→+  ratingCount?: number;
  3717→+  defaultUnitPrices: UnitPrice[];
  3718→+  defaultQuoteQty: number;
  3719→+  primaryCategoryId?: number;
  3720→+};
  3721→+
  3722→+export type ProductsCarouselWidgetProps = {
  3723→+  products: TrendingProduct[];
  3724→+  /** @default false */
  3725→+  isLoading?: boolean;
  3726→+  /** @default 'Trending Products' */
  3727→+  title?: string;
  3728→+  /**
  3729→+   * Fired once when the widget scrolls into the viewport.
  3730→+   * Receives all loaded products so the caller can build any analytics payload.
  3731→+   */
  3732→+  onWidgetViewed?: (products: TrendingProduct[]) => void;
  3733→+  /**
  3734→+   * Fired when a product card is clicked.
  3735→+   * Receives the full product and its 0-based index for analytics.
  3736→+   */
  3737→+  onCardClicked?: (product: TrendingProduct, index: number) => void;
  3738→+  /**
  3739→+   * Render prop: inject a FavoriteButton from the consuming app.
  3740→+   * Rendered as an absolute overlay in the top-right of the product image.
  3741→+   * The consuming app owns favorites state and wraps the widget with FavoritesProvider.
  3742→+   */
  3743→+  renderFavoriteButton?: (product: TrendingProduct) => React.ReactNode;
  3744→+  /**
  3745→+   * Render prop: inject custom price display (e.g. ProductCardPriceDetails from catalog).
  3746→+   * Replaces the default price and the built-in Pricing Details popover.
  3747→+   * Falls back to a simple price derived from defaultUnitPrices when omitted.
  3748→+   */
  3749→+  renderPriceDetails?: (product: TrendingProduct) => React.ReactNode;
  3750→+  /**
  3751→+   * Rendered below the carousel. Intended for supplemental content such as a "View all" link.
  3752→+   * Not rendered during the loading state.
  3753→+   */
  3754→+  children?: React.ReactNode;
  3755→+  /**
  3756→+   * Override default slides config per breakpoint.
  3757→+   * Merged with defaults — only the breakpoints you specify are overridden.
  3758→+   */
  3759→+  slidesConfig?: Partial<Record<Breakpoint, { perView: number; spacing: number }>>;
  3760→+  className?: string;
  3761→+  style?: React.CSSProperties;
  3762→+};
  3763→diff --git a/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/utils.test.ts b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/utils.test.ts
  3764→new file mode 100644
  3765→index 000000000..5b792b23a
  3766→--- /dev/null
  3767→+++ b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/utils.test.ts
  3768→@@ -0,0 +1,79 @@
  3769→+import { formatPrice, formatRatingCount, formatRatingScore } from './utils';
  3770→+import type { UnitPrice } from './types';
  3771→+
  3772→+describe('formatPrice', () => {
  3773→+  it('returns null for empty prices array', () => {
  3774→+    expect(formatPrice([], 25)).toBeNull();
  3775→+  });
  3776→+
  3777→+  it('returns amount and suffix for the matching quantity', () => {
  3778→+    const prices: UnitPrice[] = [
  3779→+      { quantity: 12, price: 11.95 },
  3780→+      { quantity: 25, price: 9.5 },
  3781→+    ];
  3782→+    expect(formatPrice(prices, 25)).toEqual({ amount: '$9.50', suffix: '/ea for 25 items' });
  3783→+  });
  3784→+
  3785→+  it('falls back to first entry when quoteQty has no match', () => {
  3786→+    const prices: UnitPrice[] = [{ quantity: 6, price: 14.0 }];
  3787→+    expect(formatPrice(prices, 100)).toEqual({ amount: '$14.00', suffix: '/ea for 6 items' });
  3788→+  });
  3789→+
  3790→+  it('does not include originalAmount when there is no discount', () => {
  3791→+    const prices: UnitPrice[] = [{ quantity: 25, price: 9.5 }];
  3792→+    const result = formatPrice(prices, 25);
  3793→+    expect(result?.originalAmount).toBeUndefined();
  3794→+  });
  3795→+
  3796→+  it('does not include originalAmount when regularPrice equals price', () => {
  3797→+    const prices: UnitPrice[] = [{ quantity: 25, price: 9.5, regularPrice: 9.5 }];
  3798→+    const result = formatPrice(prices, 25);
  3799→+    expect(result?.originalAmount).toBeUndefined();
  3800→+  });
  3801→+
  3802→+  it('includes originalAmount when regularPrice is greater than price', () => {
  3803→+    const prices: UnitPrice[] = [{ quantity: 25, price: 8.07, regularPrice: 9.5 }];
  3804→+    expect(formatPrice(prices, 25)).toEqual({
  3805→+      amount: '$8.07',
  3806→+      suffix: '/ea for 25 items',
  3807→+      originalAmount: '$9.50',
  3808→+    });
  3809→+  });
  3810→+
  3811→+  it('uses the discounted entry when quoteQty matches a discounted price', () => {
  3812→+    const prices: UnitPrice[] = [
  3813→+      { quantity: 12, price: 10.0, regularPrice: 12.0 },
  3814→+      { quantity: 25, price: 8.0 },
  3815→+    ];
  3816→+    expect(formatPrice(prices, 12)).toEqual({
  3817→+      amount: '$10.00',
  3818→+      suffix: '/ea for 12 items',
  3819→+      originalAmount: '$12.00',
  3820→+    });
  3821→+  });
  3822→+});
  3823→+
  3824→+describe('formatRatingScore', () => {
  3825→+  it('formats score to one decimal', () => {
  3826→+    expect(formatRatingScore(4.678)).toBe('4.7');
  3827→+  });
  3828→+
  3829→+  it('clamps scores below 1 to 1.0', () => {
  3830→+    expect(formatRatingScore(0)).toBe('1.0');
  3831→+    expect(formatRatingScore(0.5)).toBe('1.0');
  3832→+  });
  3833→+});
  3834→+
  3835→+describe('formatRatingCount', () => {
  3836→+  it('formats count with parentheses', () => {
  3837→+    expect(formatRatingCount(448)).toBe('(448)');
  3838→+  });
  3839→+
  3840→+  it('caps at 10,000 with + suffix', () => {
  3841→+    expect(formatRatingCount(13135)).toBe('(10,000+)');
  3842→+  });
  3843→+
  3844→+  it('returns null for NaN', () => {
  3845→+    expect(formatRatingCount(NaN)).toBeNull();
  3846→+  });
  3847→+});
  3848→diff --git a/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/utils.ts b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/utils.ts
  3849→new file mode 100644
  3850→index 000000000..a32e8cfdc
  3851→--- /dev/null
  3852→+++ b/packages/extensions/reco/ProductsCarouselWidget/utils.ts
  3853→@@ -0,0 +1,74 @@
  3854→+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
  3855→+import units from '@customink/design-tokens/semantics/units';
  3856→+import type { Breakpoint, UnitPrice } from './types';
  3857→+
  3858→+const BREAKPOINTS = {
  3859→+  mobile: parseInt(units.size.breakpoint.tablet, 10),
  3860→+  desktop: parseInt(units.size.breakpoint.desktop, 10),
  3861→+};
  3862→+
  3863→+function getBreakpoint(width: number): Breakpoint {
  3864→+  if (width < BREAKPOINTS.mobile) return 'mobile';
  3865→+  if (width < BREAKPOINTS.desktop) return 'tablet';
  3866→+  return 'desktop';
  3867→+}
  3868→+
  3869→+export const DEFAULT_SLIDES_CONFIG: Record<Breakpoint, { perView: number; spacing: number }> = {
  3870→+  mobile: { perView: 2.25, spacing: 8 },
  3871→+  tablet: { perView: 4, spacing: 16 },
  3872→+  desktop: { perView: 5, spacing: 16 },
  3873→+};
  3874→+
  3875→+export function getSlidesConfig(
  3876→+  breakpoint: Breakpoint,
  3877→+  overrides?: Partial<Record<Breakpoint, { perView: number; spacing: number }>>,
  3878→+): { perView: number; spacing: number } {
  3879→+  return { ...DEFAULT_SLIDES_CONFIG[breakpoint], ...overrides?.[breakpoint] };
  3880→+}
  3881→+
  3882→+export function useBreakpoint(): Breakpoint {
  3883→+  const [breakpoint, setBreakpoint] = useState<Breakpoint>(() =>
  3884→+    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? getBreakpoint(window.innerWidth) : 'desktop',
  3885→+  );
  3886→+
  3887→+  useEffect(() => {
  3888→+    const mqMobile = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${BREAKPOINTS.mobile - 1}px)`);
  3889→+    const mqTablet = window.matchMedia(`(min-width: ${BREAKPOINTS.mobile}px) and (max-width: ${BREAKPOINTS.desktop - 1}px)`);
  3890→+    const handler = () => setBreakpoint(getBreakpoint(window.innerWidth));
  3891→+    mqMobile.addEventListener('change', handler);
  3892→+    mqTablet.addEventListener('change', handler);
  3893→+    return () => {
  3894→+      mqMobile.removeEventListener('change', handler);
  3895→+      mqTablet.removeEventListener('change', handler);
  3896→+    };
  3897→+  }, []);
  3898→+
  3899→+  return breakpoint;
  3900→+}
  3901→+
  3902→+export function formatRatingScore(score: number): string {
  3903→+  const adjusted = score < 1 ? 1 : score;
  3904→+  return adjusted.toFixed(1);
  3905→+}
  3906→+
  3907→+export function formatRatingCount(count: number): string | null {
  3908→+  if (Number.isNaN(count)) return null;
  3909→+  const cap = 10_000;
  3910→+  const capped = count > cap ? cap : count;
  3911→+  const formatted = capped.toLocaleString();
  3912→+  return `(${count > cap ? `${formatted}+` : formatted})`;
  3913→+}
  3914→+
  3915→+export function formatPrice(
  3916→+  unitPrices: UnitPrice[],
  3917→+  quoteQty: number,
  3918→+): { amount: string; suffix: string; originalAmount?: string } | null {
  3919→+  if (!unitPrices.length) return null;
  3920→+  const match = unitPrices.find((p) => p.quantity === quoteQty) ?? unitPrices[0];
  3921→+  const isDiscounted = match.regularPrice != null && match.regularPrice > match.price;
  3922→+  return {
  3923→+    amount: `$${match.price.toFixed(2)}`,
  3924→+    suffix: `/ea for ${match.quantity} items`,
  3925→+    ...(isDiscounted && match.regularPrice != null && { originalAmount: `$${match.regularPrice.toFixed(2)}` }),
  3926→+  };
  3927→+}

  3956→+      matches: false,

  3961→+  });
  3962→+  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'IntersectionObserver', {

  3968→+describe('TrendingProductsCarousel', () => {

  3986→+  });
  3987→+
  3988→+  it('passes hook options to useTrendingProductsByCategory', () => {
  3989→+    mockHook.mockReturnValue({ products: [], isLoading: false, isError: false });
  3990→+    render(
  3991→+      <TrendingProductsCarousel
  3992→+        categoryId={574}
  3993→+        limit={6}
  3994→+        excludeProductIds={[123]}
  3995→+        imageDesignCid="abc123"
  3996→+        title="Trending"
  3997→+      />,
  3998→+    );
  3999→+    expect(mockHook).toHaveBeenCalledWith(

  4001→+    );
  4002→+  });
  4003→+
  4004→+  it('calls onError when the fetch fails', () => {
  4005→+    mockHook.mockReturnValue({ products: [], isLoading: false, isError: true });
  4006→+    const onError = jest.fn();

  4010→+
  4011→+  it('does not call onError when there is no error', () => {

  4017→+
  4018→+  it('forwards ref to the root element', () => {

  4025→diff --git a/packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsCarousel/TrendingProductsCarousel.tsx b/packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsCarousel/TrendingProductsCarousel.tsx
  4026→new file mode 100644
  4027→index 000000000..f63159090
  4028→--- /dev/null
  4029→+++ b/packages/extensions/reco/TrendingProductsCarousel/TrendingProductsCarousel.tsx
  4030→@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
  4031→+import React from 'react';
  4032→+import { useTrendingProductsByCategory } from '../useTrendingProductsByCategory';
  4033→+import type { UseTrendingProductsByCategoryOptions } from '../useTrendingProductsByCategory';
  4034→+import { ProductsCarouselWidget } from '../ProductsCarouselWidget';
  4035→+import type { ProductsCarouselWidgetProps } from '../ProductsCarouselWidget/types';
  4036→+
  4037→+export type TrendingProductsCarouselProps = UseTrendingProductsByCategoryOptions &
  4038→+  Omit<ProductsCarouselWidgetProps, 'products' | 'isLoading'> & {
  4039→+    /** Called when the recommendations fetch fails. Use for logging or showing a toast. */
  4040→+    onError?: () => void;
  4041→+  };
  4042→+
  4043→+/**
  4044→+ * Zero-config carousel for trending products.
  4045→+ * Fetches data from the recommendations service and renders ProductsCarouselWidget.
  4046→+ *
  4047→+ * Minimal usage:
  4048→+ * ```tsx
  4049→+ * <TrendingProductsCarousel categoryId={574} />
  4050→+ * ```
  4051→+ */
  4052→+export const TrendingProductsCarousel = React.forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, TrendingProductsCarouselProps>(
  4053→+  function TrendingProductsCarousel(
  4054→+    { categoryId, limit, excludeProductIds, baseUrl, enabled, imageDesignCid, onError, ...widgetProps },
  4055→+    ref,
  4056→+  ) {
  4057→+    const { products, isLoading, isError } = useTrendingProductsByCategory({
  4058→+      categoryId,
  4059→+      limit,
  4060→+      excludeProductIds,
  4061→+      baseUrl,
  4062→+      enabled,
  4063→+      imageDesignCid,
  4064→+    });
  4065→+
  4066→+    React.useEffect(() => {
  4067→+      if (isError) onError?.();
  4068→+    }, [isError, onError]);
  4069→+
  4070→+    return <ProductsCarouselWidget ref={ref} products={products} isLoading={isLoading} {...widgetProps} />;
  4071→+  },
  4072→+);
  4073→+
  4074→+TrendingProductsCarousel.displayName = 'TrendingProductsCarousel';

  4081→+export { TrendingProductsCarousel } from './TrendingProductsCarousel';
  4082→+export type { TrendingProductsCarouselProps } from './TrendingProductsCarousel';

  4095→+  });
  4096→+});
  4097→+
  4098→+describe('processImageUrl', () => {
  4099→+  it('returns undefined for null', () => {
  4100→+    expect(processImageUrl(null, 'abc123')).toBeUndefined();
  4101→+  });
  4102→+
  4103→+  it('returns undefined for undefined', () => {
  4104→+    expect(processImageUrl(undefined, 'abc123')).toBeUndefined();
  4105→+  });
  4106→+
  4107→+  it('returns undefined for empty string', () => {
  4108→+    expect(processImageUrl('', 'abc123')).toBeUndefined();
  4109→+  });
  4110→+
  4111→+  it('returns the original string when URL parsing fails', () => {
  4112→+    expect(processImageUrl('not-a-url', 'abc123')).toBe('not-a-url');
  4113→+  });
  4114→+
  4115→+  it('sets the design param to the provided CID', () => {
  4116→+    const result = processImageUrl('https://example.com/image.jpg', 'abc123');
  4117→+    expect(result).toBeDefined();
  4118→+    expect(new URL(result as string).searchParams.get('design')).toBe('abc123');
  4119→+  });
  4120→+
  4121→+  it('replaces an existing design param', () => {
  4122→+    const result = processImageUrl('https://example.com/image.jpg?design=old', 'new123');
  4123→+    expect(result).toBeDefined();
  4124→+    expect(new URL(result as string).searchParams.get('design')).toBe('new123');
  4125→+  });
  4126→+
  4127→+  it('adds all placement params', () => {
  4128→+    const result = processImageUrl('https://example.com/image.jpg', 'abc123');
  4129→+    expect(result).toBeDefined();
  4130→+    const url = new URL(result as string);
  4131→+    expect(url.searchParams.get('placeMax')).toBe('1');
  4132→+    expect(url.searchParams.get('placeMaxPct')).toBe('0.8');
  4133→+    expect(url.searchParams.get('placeUseProduct')).toBe('1');
  4134→+    expect(url.searchParams.get('placeUseView')).toBe('front');
  4135→+  });
  4136→+
  4137→+  it('preserves unrelated query params', () => {
  4138→+    const result = processImageUrl('https://example.com/image.jpg?ixw=270&ixq=60', 'abc123');
  4139→+    expect(result).toBeDefined();
  4140→+    const url = new URL(result as string);
  4141→+    expect(url.searchParams.get('ixw')).toBe('270');
  4142→+    expect(url.searchParams.get('ixq')).toBe('60');
  4143→+  });
  4144→+
  4145→+  it('works with DEFAULT_RECOMMENDATIONS_DESIGN_CID', () => {

  4153→index 000000000..3335e0605

  4156→@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
  4157→+/** Default YDH logo design used for recommendation product images */
  4158→+export const DEFAULT_RECOMMENDATIONS_DESIGN_CID = 'djn0-00by-wu36';
  4159→+
  4160→+/**
  4161→+ * Applies logo-placement query params to a product image URL.
  4162→+ * @param designCid - Design CID to embed (e.g. `DEFAULT_RECOMMENDATIONS_DESIGN_CID` or a real design CID).
  4163→+ */
  4164→+export function processImageUrl(url: string | null | undefined, designCid: string): string | undefined {
  4165→+  if (!url) return undefined;
  4166→+  try {
  4167→+    const imageUrl = new URL(url);
  4168→+    imageUrl.searchParams.delete('design');
  4169→+    imageUrl.searchParams.set('design', designCid);
  4170→+    imageUrl.searchParams.set('placeMax', '1');
  4171→+    imageUrl.searchParams.set('placeMaxPct', '0.8');
  4172→+    imageUrl.searchParams.set('placeUseProduct', '1');
  4173→+    imageUrl.searchParams.set('placeUseView', 'front');
  4174→+    return imageUrl.toString();
  4175→+  } catch {
  4176→+    return url;
  4177→+  }
  4178→+}

  4204→+export function mapUnitPrice(p: ApiUnitPrice): UnitPrice {
  4205→+  const price = parseFloat(p.price);
  4206→+  const regularPrice = p.regular_unit_price != null ? parseFloat(p.regular_unit_price) : null;
  4207→+  return {
  4208→+    quantity: p.quantity,
  4209→+    price,
  4210→+    ...(regularPrice != null && regularPrice > price && { regularPrice }),
  4211→+  };
  4212→+}
  4213→+
  4214→+export function mapProduct(p: ApiRecoProduct, { imageDesignCid }: MapProductOptions = {}): TrendingProduct {
  4215→+  const ratingScore = typeof p.rating_score === 'number' ? p.rating_score : parseFloat(p.rating_score);
  4216→+  const ratingCount = parseInt(p.rating_count, 10);
  4217→+
  4218→+  return {
  4219→+    styleId: p.style_id,
  4220→+    name: p.name,
  4221→+    link: p.link,
  4222→+    imageSrc: imageDesignCid ? processImageUrl(p.image_src, imageDesignCid) ?? p.image_src : p.image_src,
  4223→+    minQty: p.min_qty,
  4224→+    ratingScore: ratingScore > 0 ? ratingScore : undefined,
  4225→+    ratingCount: ratingCount > 0 ? ratingCount : undefined,
  4226→+    defaultUnitPrices: (p.default_unit_prices ?? []).map(mapUnitPrice),
  4227→+    defaultQuoteQty: p.default_quote_qty,
  4228→+    primaryCategoryId: p.primary_category_id ?? undefined,
  4229→+  };
  4230→+}

I've read through the full PR diff. Here's the review:

---

## Code Review: PR #1094 — TrendingProductsCarousel Migration

Context: new `@customink/pigment-extensions/reco` package, no existing consumers, risk is low. Review focuses on correctness, React patterns, and API design.

---

### 🔴 HIGH

**1. Wrong ARIA role on Popover (`Popover.tsx:2543`)**
```tsx
<span ref={panelRef} role="dialog" aria-hidden={!visible} ...>
```
`role="dialog"` implies a modal with focus trap and requires `aria-labelledby`/`aria-label`. A hover/focus-triggered pricing panel is a `role="tooltip"`. The trigger should have `aria-describedby={panelId}`. As-is, screen readers announce this incorrectly and it fails WCAG.

**2. Side effect during render breaks React Strict Mode (`decorator.tsx:35-36`)**
```tsx
const originalFetchRef = React.useRef(window.fetch);
window.fetch = async (...) => { ... }; // ← happens during render
```
In React Strict Mode (dev), components render twice. On second render, `originalFetchRef.current` is already the patched fetch, so cleanup restores the patched version. The mock leaks. This should be in a `useLayoutEffect` with the patching logic, using a flag to guard against double invocation.

**3. `ref` not forwarded in loading state (`ProductsCarouselWidget.tsx:3639`)**
```tsx
if (isLoading) return <LoadingSkeleton count={Math.floor(perView)} gap={spacing} />;
```
`ref` is never passed to `LoadingSkeleton`, so `ref.current` is `null` during loading. If a consumer passes a ref expecting to track the widget root (e.g. for an IntersectionObserver), it silently fails. Either forward the ref to `LoadingSkeleton` or wrap both branches in the same root div.

---

### 🟡 MEDIUM

**4. Inline lambdas in `.map()` (`ProductsCarouselWidget.tsx:3666–3670`)**
```tsx
// eslint-disable-next-line react/jsx-no-bind
onClick={() => onCardClicked?.(product, index)}
// eslint-disable-next-line react/jsx-no-bind
renderPriceDetails={renderPriceDetails ? () => renderPriceDetails(product) : undefined}
```
Two new function instances are created per product per render. The ESLint disables acknowledge this is known but leave it unfixed. With 10+ cards in the carousel, this is 20+ new allocations every render. Extract via `useCallback` or lift into a `ProductCard` wrapper component that takes `product` as prop and closes over it stably.

**5. `arrowPath` defined inside render (`Carousel.tsx:2147`)**
```tsx
const arrowPath = 'M8.29289 5.70711C7.90237...';
```
New string allocation on every render. Hoist to module level.

**6. `widgetRef` is an inner element, not the root (`ProductsCarouselWidget.tsx:3616–3646`)**
```tsx
const widgetRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null); // inner div
// ...
<div ref={ref} className={rootClass} style={style}>   // outer — consumer's ref
  <div ref={widgetRef}>                               // inner — IntersectionObserver target
```
The `onWidgetViewed` effect observes `widgetRef` (inner div, excludes the margin from `.root`). The `IntersectionObserver` threshold `0.1` may fire before the card row is visible due to the `margin: 2.5rem 0` on the outer div. Attach the observer to `ref` (outer) or the outer div directly.

**7. Hardcoded `width: 332px` (`ProductCardPriceDetailsContent.module.css:2727`)**
```css
.root {
  width: 332px;
}
```
Magic pixel value in a design library component. Should be a CSS custom property (`--price-details-popover-width`) or at minimum a comment citing the Figma source (`/* Figma: Pricing Popover / Width */`). The value 332 is currently untracked.

---

### 🔵 LOW

**8. `imageSrcSet` never populated by mapper (`mapper.ts:4214`)**
`TrendingProduct.imageSrcSet` exists on the type but `ApiRecoProduct` has no corresponding field, so it's always `undefined` after mapping. Either remove the field from the type or add `image_src_set?: string` to `ApiRecoProduct`. Storybook uses `imageSrcSet` from `MOCK_PRODUCTS` populated manually, masking this gap.

**9. Duplicate `@keyframes shimmer` definitions**
`LoadingSkeleton.module.css:2193` and `ProductCardPriceDetailsContent.module.css:2717` both define `@keyframes shimmer` with slightly different directions (`-200% → 200%` vs `200% → -200%`). CSS Modules scope the names, so they don't conflict, but if the animation direction is intentionally different, it should be documented. If unintentional, extract to a shared `shimmer.module.css`.

**10. PR title typo**
"Reco 730 trendng products migration" → missing `i` in "trending".

---

### ✅ What's solid

- Clean Atoms/Molecules/Widget separation with correct public API boundary (atoms not exported)
- `forwardRef` + `displayName` on every component — good
- Render prop pattern for `renderFavoriteButton` / `renderPriceDetails` decouples catalog-specific state correctly
- Lazy mount pattern in `Popover` (`mounted` state + `createPortal`) — correct
- `useBreakpoint` correctly computes from `window.innerWidth` in the handler, not from the MQ result — avoids the skip-breakpoint edge case
- `hasFiredViewEvent` ref prevents double-firing `onWidgetViewed` — correct
- Test coverage is thorough and uses real mock data
